## ./plan
const PLAN_AU_KM: 149597870.7
const PLAN_FONT: "10px system-ui,-apple-system,'Segoe UI','Hiragino Sans','Noto Sans JP',sans-serif"
const PLAN_SYSTEMS: readonly PlanSystem[]
export interface Plan { system: PlanSystem; time: number; spacing: PlanSpacing; unit: "au" | "radii"; extent: number; inner: number; centre: PlanDot; dots: PlanDot[]; orbits: PlanOrbit[]; marked: string | undefined; marks: readonly string[]; }
export interface PlanDot { id: string; kind: PlanKind; x: number; y: number; distance: number; marked: boolean; beyond: boolean; }
export interface PlanDrawOptions { size?: number; lang?: PlanLang; names?: boolean; id?: string; measure?: PlanMeasure; }
export interface PlanExtra { id: string; au: Vec3; kind?: PlanKind; }
export type PlanKind = "star" | "planet" | "dwarf-planet" | "moon" | "asteroid" | "centaur" | "comet" | "spacecraft";
export type PlanLang = "en" | "ja";
export type PlanMeasure = (text: string, bold: boolean) => number;
export interface PlanOptions { system?: PlanSystem; mark?: string; spacing?: PlanSpacing; extras?: readonly PlanExtra[]; marks?: readonly string[]; }
export interface PlanOrbit { id: string; marked: boolean; points: readonly (readonly [ number, number ])[]; }
export type PlanSpacing = "squeezed" | "true";
export type PlanSystem = "sun" | keyof typeof MOONS_OF;

## ./comets
const COMET_ACCURACY: { readonly read: "2026-11-11"; readonly source: "NASA/JPL Small-Body Database, https://ssd-api.jpl.nasa.gov/sbdb.api"; readonly trustedDays: 365; readonly note: "The orbit is two-body motion on the elements of one epoch: the planets' pull and a comet's own jets move the real orbit away from it, so a position is good near the epoch and drifts away from it, by days or years later a comet's closest approach to the Sun may be off. A long-period comet is good only near its epoch; a periodic comet's last and next perihelion are JPL's own dates inside the span they cover and are repeated from the osculating period outside it. For a close look at one date use JPL Horizons."; }

const APPROACH_ALBEDO: { readonly dark: 0.05; readonly bright: 0.25; }
const APPROACH_NOTE: { readonly fetched: "2026-11-11"; readonly text: "These are predictions from the orbits JPL knew on 2026-11-11. An approach changes as the orbit of the body is refined by new observations, and a body found after that day is not here: nearly all the small ones are found only days before they pass. The distance is between the centres of the body and the Earth; the uncertainty of the moment and the least and greatest distances are JPL's 3-sigma values. A size from the absolute magnitude assumes an albedo between 0.05 and 0.25."; readonly coverage: { readonly wide: { readonly from: "2026-01-01"; readonly to: "2036-12-31"; readonly maxAu: 0.05; }; readonly close: { readonly from: "1900-01-01"; readonly to: "2025-12-31"; readonly maxAu: 0.002; }; }; }
